# Task board (Lead writes; workers claim one row; verifier records the verdict)

Drive limit (lead 2026-10-18): the Google Drive connector downloads files up to 10 MB only; larger private files must be on Drive as .zip.
Parallel team (owner, 2026-10-18): 3 workers hourly, verifier hourly, lead every 2 h; 8-12 TODO, no two CLAIMED tasks on one engine file (TEAM.md "Parallel work").
Plan, milestones and daily targets: PLAN.md (owner, 2026-10-18). The engine is the product; Alsenan is one sample.
Status: QUEUED -> TODO -> CLAIMED (worker, branch) -> READY (worker done) -> MERGED or REJECTED (verifier, reason).
BLOCKED-DATA = needs `urban-boq-private-data`; BLOCKED-OWNER = needs an answer in OWNER_QUESTIONS.md.
Order: lowest gate first, then money at risk.

## Ideas (from the research scout; the lead turns useful ones into tasks)

From the 2026-10-17 BOQ engines study, which the owner supplied (see RESEARCH_LOG.md and research/2026-10-17_BOQ_ENGINES_STUDY.md). Ranked for the Alsenan scoreboard:

1. Visible deduction rows per wall (`D1 2 x 1.0 x 2.1 = -4.20 m2`, tied to the DXF block): makes blockwork and plaster gaps explainable.
2. Measured, waste-adjusted and order quantity as three columns: shows whether a gap comes from measurement, the waste rule or rounding.
3. Net, gross and centreline variants per room or wall, side by side, matching whichever basis the freelancer used.
4. Recipes per unit (block wall per m2 expands to blocks, glue, lintels, plaster and labour) in one rule source.
5. Provenance on every quantity: file, layer, entity handle, rule version, reviewed.
6. Overlap check: shared floor = sum - union must be 0.
7. Three-band confidence: commit, withhold with location (an owner question), or reject.
8. Skirting = perimeter minus stated door widths, with the inputs recorded.
9. DXF revision diff that flags affected BOQ lines (needed for the second villa).
10. (scout 2026-10-17) Rebar: link every schedule value to (element, location, parameter) and hold it for review before tonnage (Li et al. 2026; T-009).
11. (scout 2026-10-17) Rebar: bar-mark and count-expression parser tested on dxfstructure notation (`[1] - #12 L=2120`, `2*2#12-[4]`); own code only, GPL source (T-009).
12. (scout 2026-10-17) Blockwork: sandbox test of pygeoops.centerline (BSD-3) on wall polygons as an independent wall-length check (T-007).
13. (scout 2026-10-17) Blockwork / finishes: girth diagnostic, external girth - 4T vs engine external-wall centreline, per floor (K.C. Tang centre-line rules).
14. (scout 2026-10-18) DWG: sandbox-test ACadSharp (MIT) as a second DWG decoder: P7757 / ST7757 DWG -> DXF 2018, diff against LibreDWG JSON; unblocks Q-11 and villa 2 if they agree.
15. (scout 2026-10-18) Concrete Q-12: term-by-term hand-calc sheet (stair waist / steps / landing; dome outer minus inner spherical cap + ring beam; pool slab + each wall) to settle the 3 UNRESOLVED lines.
16. (scout 2026-10-18) Wet / dry T-028: context score (area, doors, adjacency to bedroom / other wet room, fixtures) proposes a type on the HELD record only (ResPlan room-graph idea; do not load its pickle).
17. (scout 2026-10-20, R-01) Scanned elevations: release an OCR'd dimension chain only if its parts sum to the overall dimension or the bracketing level-mark difference (chain-sum check, Tsinghua dimension-set method); Tesseract (Apache-2.0) digit whitelist + 90 deg re-read, PaddleOCR (Apache-2.0) as second reader in sandbox; check-only against the T-083 hand table.
18. (scout 2026-10-20, R-02) Arabic SHX: render the client's own SHX font to a numbered glyph sheet with shxparser or @mlightcad/shx-parser (both MIT), map codes to Unicode presentation forms from that sheet (font-fact file keyed by font SHA-256), NFKC to base letters; unmapped codes stay UNREAD.
19. (scout 2026-10-20, R-05a) Members in INSERTs / open outlines: ezdxf virtual_entities (mirrors fine) + ezdxf 1.4 edgeminer / edgesmith loops with a reported gap_tol (strict pass, then tolerant pass with "gap bridged" evidence), classify by geometry + mark, not by layer.
20. (scout 2026-10-20, R-05b) Membrane rolls: strips as items, roll as bin; first-fit-decreasing with the L1 = ceil(sum / roll length) proof, OR-Tools CP-SAT (Apache-2.0) only when FFD > L1; print the cutting plan; laps and roll size as product inputs.
21. (scout 2026-10-20) Policy: vision-language models are evidence for printed text only, never for door / window counts (AECV-Bench: 0.40-0.55 counting accuracy).
22. (scout 2026-10-20, R-06) Scanned notes: frame + long-line removal, connected-component text blocks in note boxes / room outlines; release only when two readings agree AND match a closed list (tile formats, room lexicon, door-height band); Tesseract `ara` data (Apache-2.0) needs an image change; Arabic free text stays HELD (KITAB-Bench CER 0.54-0.79 for permissive engines).
23. (scout 2026-10-20, R-06) Sandbox: PaddleOCR arabic_PP-OCRv5_mobile_rec (Apache-2.0, inference export only, no .pdparams pickle) as the second engine against the T-091 hand census.
24. (scout 2026-10-20, R-07) Membrane: strip length = projection of outline ∩ full strip rectangle (not one cross-section); prove coverage with shapely difference(grid_size=0.001); any residue must be a declared, counted patch; random rectilinear outline generator incl. step parallel to strips.
25. (scout 2026-10-20, R-08) Blockwork: pair-midline centreline per thickness class, WAG junction closure (Domínguez 2012), exact check area / t = Σ L − t/2 per T-stem − t per X; piers < 4 t as linear items; NRM2 centre-line basis.
